| `models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/pdfs/1992_Luettich02.pdf` PDF 쪽 23 / 인쇄 쪽 17<br>식 번호 없음. 대조 ID `PDF-TIDAL-L0`<br>`L_o=3\sin^2(\phi)-1;\quad L_1=\sin(2\phi);\quad L_2=\cos^2(\phi)`<br>판독 기록: `_staging/recovery/read/ADCIRC/manuals/pdfs/1992_Luettich02.pdf/p019-036.md:19` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:297` `C..  Corrected long term species (unfortunately there is a mistake in old ADCIRC `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:298` `C..  theory report that also propagated into some subsequent publications)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:303` `            L_N(0,I) = 1.5d0*COS(SFEA(I))*COS(SFEA(I)) - 1d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:305` `            L_N(1,I) = SIN(2d0*SFEA(I))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:307` `            L_N(2,I) = COS(SFEA(I))*COS(SFEA(I))` | 불일치 | 해석: 일주·반일주 위도 함수는 같다. 장주기 코드값은 1.5cos²φ−1이다. 해석: 코드값은 원문 L₀의 −1/2이다. 같은 분조 진폭을 넣으면 장주기 항의 부호와 크기가 다르다. 코드 주석도 old theory report의 오류를 명시한다. 문서는 Report 1, November 1992이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |
| `models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/pdfs/1992_Luettich02.pdf` PDF 쪽 23 / 인쇄 쪽 17<br>식 번호 없음. 대조 ID `PDF-TIDAL-ALPHA`<br>`\alpha=0.69;\qquad\alpha=1`<br>문서 원문: `For reference, α = 1 would correspond to a perfectly rigid sphere.`<br>판독 기록: `_staging/recovery/read/ADCIRC/manuals/pdfs/1992_Luettich02.pdf/p019-036.md:19` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/timestep.F:1540` `                  TPMUL=RampTip*ETRF(J)*TPK(J)*FFT(J)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/tidalpotential.F90:347` `      TIPVAL = (1.d0 + self%m_k2value - self%m_h2value)*TIPVAL` | 일치 | 해석: 분조 방식은 입력 ETRF를 곱한다. ETRF=0.69 또는 1을 지정하면 원문의 해당 지구조석 감쇠 선택에 대응한다. 코드가 ETRF를 언제나 0.69로 강제하는 것은 아니다. FullTIP 경로는 별도 Love 수 입력값을 사용한다. 문서는 Report 1, November 1992이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |
| `models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/pdfs/1992_Luettich02.pdf` PDF 쪽 55 / 인쇄 쪽 49<br>식 번호 없음. 대조 ID `PDF-SOLVER-LU`<br><br>문서 원문: `is time-independent and is therefore assembled and LU decomposed only once.`<br>판독 기록: `_staging/recovery/read/ADCIRC/manuals/pdfs/1992_Luettich02.pdf/p055-072.md:15` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:427` `      NCIFBLCK: IF(NCChange.GT.0 .or. CGWCE_HDP ) THEN !if any subdomain grid has changed`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:2000` `      IF (ILump.eq.0) THEN ! default, fully consistent LHS`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:2003` `         CALL JCG(NP,MNP,MNEI,NEITAB,COEF,GWCE_LV,ETAS,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:2014` `               RDIAG = 1.0d0 / COEFDTemp(I)   ! COEFD --> COEFDTemp  08-11-2022 SB`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/itpackv.F:367` `         call itjcg (n,ndim,maxnz,jcoef,coef,u,wksp(ib1),wksp(ib2),` | 불일치 | 해석: 1992년 문서는 GWCE 행렬을 한 번 조립하고 LU 분해한다고 적는다. 현재 consistent 행렬은 JCG로 푼다. lumped 행렬은 대각 나눗셈으로 푼다. NCChange 또는 CGWCE_HDP 조건에서 행렬을 다시 조립한다. 보고서 1992년 판과 현재 코드의 풀이 전략 차이이다. 문서는 Report 1, November 1992이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |
| `models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/pdfs/1992_Luettich02.pdf` PDF 쪽 54 / 인쇄 쪽 48<br>식 번호 없음. 대조 ID `PDF-GRID-QUADRILATERAL`<br><br>문서 원문: `The horizontal discretization for ADCIRC has been implemented with three-node linear triangles and four-node bilinear quadrilaterals.`<br>판독 기록: `_staging/recovery/read/ADCIRC/manuals/pdfs/1992_Luettich02.pdf/p037-054.md:30` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:1630` `           FDXE(1,I) = -Y3mY2 ; !c (Y(NM2)-Y(NM3))*SFacAvg !b1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:1635` `           FDYE(3,I) =  X2mX1 ; !c  X(NM2)-X(NM1) !a3`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1007` `         NM1=NM(IE,1)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1008` `         NM2=NM(IE,2)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1009` `         NM3=NM(IE,3)` | 불일치 | 해석: 보고서는 삼각형과 4절점 쌍선형 사각형을 함께 지원한다고 적는다. 현재 주 조립은 세 꼭짓점 NM1·NM2·NM3의 삼각형을 사용한다. 이 주 코드에서 사각형 조립을 찾지 못했다. 보고서 1992년 판과 현재 코드의 수평 이산화 차이이다. 문서는 Report 1, November 1992이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |

### 2.3 1993_Westerink02.pdf


#### Chapter 2 지배방정식

| 문서 식·PDF 쪽·인쇄 쪽·원문 LaTeX 전사 | 구현 코드 또는 부재 판정용 비교 코드 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/pdfs/1993_Westerink02.pdf` PDF 쪽 15 / 인쇄 쪽 3<br>식 `1`<br>`\displaystyle \frac{\partial \zeta}{\partial t}+\frac{1}{R\cos\varphi}\left[\frac{\partial UH}{\partial\lambda}+\frac{\partial(VH\cos\varphi)}{\partial\varphi}\right]=0 \qquad (1)`<br>판독 기록: `_staging/recovery/read/ADCIRC/manuals/pdfs/1993_Westerink02.pdf/p001-018.md:27` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:5183` `              SFMX(I) = cos(SFEA0)/COS(SFEAV(I)) ;`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/mesh.F:5186` `              TANPHI(I) = (TAN(SFEAV(I))/Rearth) ;`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1537` `     &         (OnDiag*ESN1*SPM1 + OffDiag*(ESN2*SPM2+ESN3*SPM3))*MsFacR`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1539` `C...     Free surface terms from LHS (time levels s-1 & s)` | 일치 | 해석: 완전한 2D 구면식은 ICS=21의 cosφ 질량/유량 계량에서 비교했다. CPP 투영 뒤 x 미분 배율은 cosφ₀/cosφ이다. 곡률을 생략하는 ICS=2의 scaled Cartesian 근사와 구분했다. 문서는 Report 3, June 1993이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |
| `models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/pdfs/1993_Westerink02.pdf` PDF 쪽 15 / 인쇄 쪽 3<br>식 `2`<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial U}{\partial t}+\frac{1}{R\cos\varphi}U\frac{\partial U}{\partial\lambda}+\frac{1}{R}V\frac{\partial U}{\partial\varphi}-\left(\frac{\tan\varphi}{R}U+f\right)V=\\ &-\frac{1}{R\cos\varphi}\frac{\partial}{\partial\lambda}\left[\frac{p_s}{\rho_0}+g(\zeta-\eta)\right]+\frac{\tau_{s\lambda}}{\rho_0H}+\,-\tau_*U\end{aligned}\qquad (2)`<br>판독 기록: `_staging/recovery/read/ADCIRC/manuals/pdfs/1993_Westerink02.pdf/p001-018.md:27` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/momentum.F:586` `     &             -IFNLCT*(U1AvgDU1DXA+V1AvgDU1DYA)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/momentum.F:756` `         CorifA  = CORIF(I) + IFNLCT*TANPHI(I)*UU1(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/momentum.F:770` `         VCOEFXY = DTO2*(TKM(3,I) - CorifA)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/momentum.F:771` `         VCOEFYX = DTO2*(TKM(3,I) + CorifA)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/momentum.F:783` `         MOM_LV_X(I) = NCI*(MOM_LV_X(I)+WSX+(1.D0-VCOEFXX)*UU1(I) ` | 일치 | 해석: ICS=21·비보존형 이류·상수 선형/정규화 바닥 마찰·수평 점성 없음 조건에서 비교했다. 코드 CorifA=f+U tanφ/R를 좌변 x에는 음수로 넣는다. y에는 양수로 넣는다. 정규화 조석 포텐셜에 지구 조석 감소계수를 이미 포함했다. 문서는 Report 3, June 1993이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |

| `models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/pdfs/1993_Westerink02.pdf` PDF 쪽 115 / 인쇄 쪽 A1<br>식 `A1`<br>`\begin{aligned}\zeta(x,y,t)&=\sum_i^8 A_i(x,y)f_i(t_0)\\&\qquad\cos\left[\omega_i(t-t_0)+\nu_i(t_0)-\Psi_i^{(radians)}(x,y)\right]\end{aligned}`<br>판독 기록: `_staging/recovery/read/ADCIRC/manuals/pdfs/1993_Westerink02.pdf/p109-126.md:19` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1644` `         ARGJ=AMIG(J)*(timeh-NCYC*PER(J))+FACE(J)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1647` `            ARG=ARGJ-EFA(J,I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1649` `            Eta2(NBDI)=Eta2(NBDI)+EMO(J,I)*RFF*COS(ARG)` | 일치 | 해석: 8개 성분의 조화 수위 재합성은 경계 수위 합과 대응한다. NTIF/경계 성분 수가 8이고 RampElev=1인 조건에서 비교했다. 성분별 진폭·위상은 입력값이다. 문서는 Report 3, June 1993이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |
| `models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/pdfs/1993_Westerink02.pdf` PDF 쪽 116 / 인쇄 쪽 A2<br>식 `A2`<br>`\textit{Greenwich epoch}\ \Psi_i=\kappa_i+pL`<br>판독 기록: `_staging/recovery/read/ADCIRC/manuals/pdfs/1993_Westerink02.pdf/p109-126.md:20` | 비교 위치. 직접 구현 인용이 아니다.<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1647` `            ARG=ARGJ-EFA(J,I)` | 문서에만 | 해석: cotidal chart의 위상을 기준시각·Greenwich 위상으로 바꾸는 사용자 전처리 관계이다. 경계 코드는 이미 제공한 EFA를 뺀다. 이 보고서의 지역 위상 변환을 자동 실행하는 경로를 찾지 못했다. 문서는 Report 3, June 1993이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |

| `models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/pdfs/1993_Westerink02.pdf` PDF 쪽 17 / 인쇄 쪽 5<br>식 번호 없음. 대조 ID `PDF-TIDAL-L0-1993_Westerink02`<br>`\displaystyle L_0=3\sin^2\varphi-1`<br>판독 기록: `_staging/recovery/read/ADCIRC/manuals/pdfs/1993_Westerink02.pdf/p001-018.md:29` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:303` `            L_N(0,I) = 1.5d0*COS(SFEA(I))*COS(SFEA(I)) - 1d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:305` `            L_N(1,I) = SIN(2d0*SFEA(I))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:307` `            L_N(2,I) = COS(SFEA(I))*COS(SFEA(I))` | 불일치 | 해석: 이 보고서의 장주기 위도 함수도 Report 1의 3sin²φ−1을 따른다. 코드는 1.5cos²φ−1을 쓴다. 해석: 같은 진폭에서 장주기 항은 원문 대비 −1/2이다. 일주·반일주 함수의 별도 문서 표기를 이 장주기 판정으로 대신하지 않았다. 문서는 Report 3, June 1993이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |

PDF 쪽 이미지에서 중괄호 위치를 확인했다. 문서는 Report 4, August 1994이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |

| `models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/pdfs/1994_Blain03.pdf` PDF 쪽 17 / 인쇄 쪽 5<br>식 번호 없음. 대조 ID `PDF-TIDAL-L0-1994_Blain03`<br>`L_0=3\sin^2\phi-1`<br>판독 기록: `_staging/recovery/read/ADCIRC/manuals/pdfs/1994_Blain03.pdf/p001-018.md:31` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:303` `            L_N(0,I) = 1.5d0*COS(SFEA(I))*COS(SFEA(I)) - 1d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:305` `            L_N(1,I) = SIN(2d0*SFEA(I))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:307` `            L_N(2,I) = COS(SFEA(I))*COS(SFEA(I))` | 불일치 | 해석: 이 보고서의 장주기 위도 함수도 Report 1의 3sin²φ−1을 따른다. 코드는 1.5cos²φ−1을 쓴다. 해석: 같은 진폭에서 장주기 항은 원문 대비 −1/2이다. 일주·반일주 함수의 별도 문서 표기를 이 장주기 판정으로 대신하지 않았다. 문서는 Report 4, August 1994이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |

#### 무번호 주요 식과 구현 설명

| 문서 식·설명·PDF 쪽·인쇄 쪽 | 구현 코드 또는 부재 판정용 비교 코드 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/pdfs/1994_Westerink01.pdf` PDF 쪽 33 / 인쇄 쪽 21<br>식 번호 없음. 대조 ID `PDF-WETDRY-OLD`<br><br>문서 원문: `since the current implementation of ADCIRC-2DDI does not include wetting/drying elements`<br>판독 기록: `_staging/recovery/read/ADCIRC/manuals/pdfs/1994_Westerink01.pdf/p019-036.md:27` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:241` `               IF(HTOT.LE.H0) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:274` `                     NNODECODE(I) = 0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:275` `                     NODECODE(I) = 0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:1414` `            IF(NNODECODE(I).NE.NODECODE(I)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:1415` `               NODECODE(I)=NNODECODE(I)` | 불일치 | 해석: Report 2는 당시 구현에 wetting/drying 요소가 없다고 적는다. 현재 코드는 총 수심 조건에서 절점 상태를 건조로 바꾼다. 현재 H0를 입력 bathymetry만 H0로 올리는 옛 기능으로 설명할 수 없다. 보고서 1994년 판과 현재 코드의 차이이다. 현재 wetdry 내부의 다른 기능은 이 대조에 포함하지 않았다. 문서는 Report 2, January 1994이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |
| `models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/pdfs/1994_Westerink01.pdf` PDF 쪽 17 / 인쇄 쪽 5<br>식 번호 없음. 대조 ID `PDF-TIDAL-L0-1994_Westerink01`<br>`L_0=3\sin^2\phi-1`<br>판독 기록: `_staging/recovery/read/ADCIRC/manuals/pdfs/1994_Westerink01.pdf/p001-018.md:29` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:303` `            L_N(0,I) = 1.5d0*COS(SFEA(I))*COS(SFEA(I)) - 1d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:305` `            L_N(1,I) = SIN(2d0*SFEA(I))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:307` `            L_N(2,I) = COS(SFEA(I))*COS(SFEA(I))` | 불일치 | 해석: 이 보고서의 장주기 위도 함수도 Report 1의 3sin²φ−1을 따른다. 코드는 1.5cos²φ−1을 쓴다. 해석: 같은 진폭에서 장주기 항은 원문 대비 −1/2이다. 일주·반일주 함수의 별도 문서 표기를 이 장주기 판정으로 대신하지 않았다. 문서는 Report 2, January 1994이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |

| `models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/pdfs/1995_Hench.pdf` PDF 쪽 13 / 인쇄 쪽 3<br>식 `3`<br>`\displaystyle \begin{aligned}&\frac{\partial V}{\partial t}+\frac{1}{R\cos\varphi}U\frac{\partial V}{\partial\lambda}+\frac{1}{R}V\frac{\partial V}{\partial\varphi}-\left(\frac{\tan\varphi}{R}U+f\right)U=\\&-\frac{1}{R}\frac{\partial}{\partial\varphi}\left[\frac{p_s}{\rho_0}+g(\zeta-\eta)\right]+\frac{\tau_{s\varphi}}{\rho_o H}-\tau_*V\end{aligned}`<br>판독 기록: `_staging/recovery/read/ADCIRC/manuals/pdfs/1995_Hench.pdf/p001-018.md:25` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/momentum.F:771` `         VCOEFYX = DTO2*(TKM(3,I) + CorifA)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/momentum.F:785` `         MOM_LV_Y(I) = NCI*(MOM_LV_Y(I)+WSY+(1.D0-VCOEFYY)*VV1(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/momentum.F:786` `     &               - VCOEFYX*UU1(I))` | 불일치 | 해석: PDF y 운동량은 좌변 코리올리·곡률 항에 음수를 적는다. 현재 코드는 y 좌변에 +(f+U tanφ/R)U를 넣는다. 상수 f·곡률 없음 조건에서도 코리올리 부호가 다르다. PDF 쪽 이미지에서 부호를 확인했다. 문서는 Report 6, 표지 December 1994이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |

PDF 쪽 이미지에서 분수의 범위를 확인했다. 해석: 위상식의 인쇄 오류이며 현재 코드가 이를 따르지 않는다. 문서는 Report 6, 표지 December 1994이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |

PDF 쪽 이미지에서 중괄호 위치를 확인했다. PDF 마지막 곡률 시간미분도 +∂t(VH tanφ/R)로 적는다. 연속식의 −VH tanφ/R를 시간 미분하면 음수가 된다. 문서는 Report 6, 표지 December 1994이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |

#### Chapter 5 관측 대조

| `models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/pdfs/1995_Hench.pdf` PDF 쪽 15 / 인쇄 쪽 5<br>식 번호 없음. 대조 ID `PDF-TIDAL-L0-1995_Hench`<br>`L_0=3\sin^2\varphi-1`<br>판독 기록: `_staging/recovery/read/ADCIRC/manuals/pdfs/1995_Hench.pdf/p001-018.md:27` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:303` `            L_N(0,I) = 1.5d0*COS(SFEA(I))*COS(SFEA(I)) - 1d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:305` `            L_N(1,I) = SIN(2d0*SFEA(I))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:307` `            L_N(2,I) = COS(SFEA(I))*COS(SFEA(I))` | 불일치 | 해석: 이 보고서의 장주기 위도 함수도 Report 1의 3sin²φ−1을 따른다. 코드는 1.5cos²φ−1을 쓴다. 해석: 같은 진폭에서 장주기 항은 원문 대비 −1/2이다. 일주·반일주 함수의 별도 문서 표기를 이 장주기 판정으로 대신하지 않았다. 문서는 Report 6, 표지 December 1994이다. 코드는 v56.2.1 표기의 현재 원문이다. |

### 2.7 현행 rst의 식·조건·설정

원문 셀은 rst 줄을 그대로 인용한다. 식 정리 셀은 그 식의 전사 또는 비교용 변수 대응이다. 시간 가중치와 τ₀의 분석 안정성 조건은 원문의 선형화·격자·질량행렬 조건에서만 읽었다. 그 식을 실행 안정성 보장으로 바꾸지 않았다.


#### 문서 판

| 대조 ID·문서 파일:줄·원문 | 식 전사·비교용 대응 | 구현 코드 또는 비교 코드 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `RST-THEORY`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/theory/index.rst:4` `The theoretical foundation and mathematical formulation of ADCIRC is documented in the following report:`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/theory/index.rst:6` ``    `Formulation and Numerical Implementation of the 2D/3D ADCIRC Finite Element Model Version 44.XX (PDF) <https://adcirc.org/wp-content/uploads/sites/2255/2018/11/adcirc_theory_2004_12_08.pdf>`_``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/theory/index.rst:8` ``Recent updates and enhancements to the ADCIRC model's theoretical formulation are documented throughout the pages of this documentation site. Some of the updates are documented in :doc:`../user_guide/special_features/index`.`` | 독립 식 없음. 원문 조건·설정 설명을 대조했다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/vsmy.F:1010` `     &           - q(NH,1)*DT1MAlp2*TK(NH)/HsOAMB`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:516` `               GA00DPAvgOAreaIE4 = ALPHAL(IE)*A00*G*DPAvg*Cfac/AreaIE4` | 일치 | 해석: 현행 rst는 v44.XX PDF를 이론 보고서로 연결한다. 같은 rst는 최근 갱신의 일부가 다른 페이지에 있다고 적는다. 이 연결을 v56 코드와 모든 식이 같다는 주장으로 읽지 않았다. |

#### 시간 가중치

| 대조 ID·문서 파일:줄·원문 | 식 전사·비교용 대응 | 구현 코드 또는 비교 코드 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `TIME-WEIGHTS`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:8` `**A00, B00, C00** are the weighting factors (at time levels k+1, k, k-1,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:9` `respectively) for the free surface and boundary fluxes in the GWCE,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:10` `and must sum to 1. Most critically, the weighting factors are used in the`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:11` `discretization of the linear gravity wave (pressure gradient) term and are`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:12` `responsible for determining the inherent implicity (impacting solution` | `(A00,B00,C00)\leftrightarrow(k+1,k,k-1),\quad A00+B00+C00=1` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:516` `               GA00DPAvgOAreaIE4 = ALPHAL(IE)*A00*G*DPAvg*Cfac/AreaIE4`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1540` `     &        - GDPAvgOAreaIE4_S0*( C00*( SFCXADJ*FDX1*E0XGrad2A+ `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1543` `     &                                    SFCYADJ*FDY1*E1YGrad2A ) )`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1741` `     &        QFORCEJ=(QN2(1)-QN0(1))/DT2 + Tau0VAR(NBDJ)*QN1(1)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3010` `      READ(15,*) A00,B00,C00` | 불일치 | 해석: 수위의 중력항 시간 수준은 대응한다. 그러나 현행 문서는 boundary fluxes도 이 세 가중치라고 적는다. 현재 경계 τ₀Q는 QN1만 사용한다. 계수 합 1은 문서의 must 조건이다. 읽기 구문만으로 합 검사를 입증하지 않았다. |
| `TIME-TYPICAL`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:21` ``\| If the consistent mass-matrix solver is chosen (see `IM <IM>`__ parameter)``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:22` `  then a semi-implicit method is possible and encouraged. In this case the most`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:24` `` \| :math:`\mathrm{A00} = \mathrm{C00} = 0.35, \quad \mathrm{B00} = 0.30` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:26` `\| If the lumped mass-matrix solver is chosen then only an explicit method is`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:27` `  possible (the weighting A00 must be zero as no matrix solve is conducted), and`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:29` `` \| :math:`\mathrm{A00} = \mathrm{C00} = 0, \quad \mathrm{B00} = 1` `` | `A00=C00=0.35,\ B00=0.30;\qquad A00=C00=0,\ B00=1` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:2000` `      IF (ILump.eq.0) THEN ! default, fully consistent LHS`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:2003` `         CALL JCG(NP,MNP,MNEI,NEITAB,COEF,GWCE_LV,ETAS,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:2009` `      ELSE ! lumped LHS`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3010` `      READ(15,*) A00,B00,C00` | 일치 | 해석: consistent 계는 JCG를 사용한다. lumped 계는 대각 나눗셈을 사용한다. 코드가 이 권장값을 자동으로 대입한다고 판정하지 않았다. 실제 A00/B00/C00는 입력값이다. |

#### 시간 가중치 해석

| 대조 ID·문서 파일:줄·원문 | 식 전사·비교용 대응 | 구현 코드 또는 비교 코드 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `TIME-THETA`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:44` ``B00, C00 is reduced to depend on a single parameter, :math:`\theta`:``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:46` `` :math:`\mathrm{A00} = \mathrm{C00} = 0.5\theta, \quad \mathrm{B00} = 1-\theta` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:48` `In other words, k+1 and k-1 weightings are chosen to be equal. It would however`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:49` `not appear that any restriction other than the requirement that A00, B00, C00`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:50` `must sum to 1 is necessary to obtain second-order accuracy [1]_. Unconditional`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:52` ``:math:`\theta \geq 0.5`\  [1]_ [2]_. Likely because of this fact, the typical`` | `A00=C00=0.5\theta,\ B00=1-\theta,\ \theta\ge0.5` | 비교 위치. 직접 구현 인용이 아니다.<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3010` `      READ(15,*) A00,B00,C00`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:516` `               GA00DPAvgOAreaIE4 = ALPHAL(IE)*A00*G*DPAvg*Cfac/AreaIE4`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:538` `               MsFacLOnDiag =OnDiag*AreaIE*(1.d0/DT+Tau0Avg_S/2.d0)/DT/12.d0` | 문서에만 | 해석: 중앙 시간 가중치와 선형 WCE 안정성의 분석 관계이다. 코드는 θ 대신 A00/B00/C00를 직접 읽는다. θ≥0.5를 모든 비선형 모의의 무조건 안정성 보장으로 읽지 않았다. |
| `TIME-DISP`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:58` `` :math:`\theta = \frac{1}{6}\left(1 + \frac{1}{Cr^2}\right)` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:60` ``where :math:`Cr = \sqrt{gh}\Delta t/\Delta x` is the Courant number based on the``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:61` `linear gravity wave speed.` | `\theta=\frac16(1+Cr^{-2}),\quad Cr=\sqrt{gh}\Delta t/\Delta x` | 비교 위치. 직접 구현 인용이 아니다.<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3010` `      READ(15,*) A00,B00,C00`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:516` `               GA00DPAvgOAreaIE4 = ALPHAL(IE)*A00*G*DPAvg*Cfac/AreaIE4`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:538` `               MsFacLOnDiag =OnDiag*AreaIE*(1.d0/DT+Tau0Avg_S/2.d0)/DT/12.d0` | 문서에만 | 해석: 일정 수심과 규칙 격자의 분산 정확도 분석식이다. 코드가 이 최적 θ를 자동 계산하는 경로는 주 코드에서 찾지 못했다. |
| `TIME-EXPLICIT-1D-L`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:63` ``A purely explicit method (:math:`\theta = 0`) for the WCE is found to be stable``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:64` `under the following conditions [3]_ [4]_:`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:66` ``-  :math:`Cr < 1` : lumped mass-matrix solved in 1-D``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:71` `These conditions are for linear finite-elements (ADCIRC uses these) with even`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:72` `node spacings and constant bathymetry. Other conditions for quadratic` | `\theta=0,\ Cr<1` | 비교 위치. 직접 구현 인용이 아니다.<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3010` `      READ(15,*) A00,B00,C00`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:516` `               GA00DPAvgOAreaIE4 = ALPHAL(IE)*A00*G*DPAvg*Cfac/AreaIE4`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:538` `               MsFacLOnDiag =OnDiag*AreaIE*(1.d0/DT+Tau0Avg_S/2.d0)/DT/12.d0` | 문서에만 | 해석: 원문의 1D lumped·선형 요소·등간격·일정 수심 조건을 보존했다. 주 코드에서 이 해석 조건을 자동 검사하는 경로를 찾지 못했다. |
| `TIME-EXPLICIT-1D-C`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:63` ``A purely explicit method (:math:`\theta = 0`) for the WCE is found to be stable``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:64` `under the following conditions [3]_ [4]_:`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:67` ``-  :math:`Cr < \sqrt{3}/3`: consistent mass-matrix solved in 1-D``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:71` `These conditions are for linear finite-elements (ADCIRC uses these) with even`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:72` `node spacings and constant bathymetry. Other conditions for quadratic` | `\theta=0,\ Cr<\sqrt3/3` | 비교 위치. 직접 구현 인용이 아니다.<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3010` `      READ(15,*) A00,B00,C00`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:516` `               GA00DPAvgOAreaIE4 = ALPHAL(IE)*A00*G*DPAvg*Cfac/AreaIE4`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:538` `               MsFacLOnDiag =OnDiag*AreaIE*(1.d0/DT+Tau0Avg_S/2.d0)/DT/12.d0` | 문서에만 | 해석: 원문의 1D consistent·선형 요소·등간격·일정 수심 조건을 보존했다. 주 코드의 입력 읽기를 이 안정성 증명과 동일시하지 않았다. |
| `TIME-EXPLICIT-2D-L`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:63` ``A purely explicit method (:math:`\theta = 0`) for the WCE is found to be stable``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:64` `under the following conditions [3]_ [4]_:`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:68` ``-  :math:`Cr < \sqrt{2}/2`: lumped mass-matrix solved in 2-D``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:71` `These conditions are for linear finite-elements (ADCIRC uses these) with even`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:72` `node spacings and constant bathymetry. Other conditions for quadratic` | `\theta=0,\ Cr<\sqrt2/2` | 비교 위치. 직접 구현 인용이 아니다.<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3010` `      READ(15,*) A00,B00,C00`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:516` `               GA00DPAvgOAreaIE4 = ALPHAL(IE)*A00*G*DPAvg*Cfac/AreaIE4`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:538` `               MsFacLOnDiag =OnDiag*AreaIE*(1.d0/DT+Tau0Avg_S/2.d0)/DT/12.d0` | 문서에만 | 해석: 원문의 2D lumped·선형 요소·등간격·일정 수심 조건을 보존했다. 범용 비선형 격자의 필수 상한으로 바꾸지 않았다. |
| `TIME-EXPLICIT-2D-C`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:63` ``A purely explicit method (:math:`\theta = 0`) for the WCE is found to be stable``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:64` `under the following conditions [3]_ [4]_:`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:69` ``-  :math:`Cr < \sqrt{6}/6`: consistent mass-matrix solved in 2-D``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:71` `These conditions are for linear finite-elements (ADCIRC uses these) with even`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:72` `node spacings and constant bathymetry. Other conditions for quadratic` | `\theta=0,\ Cr<\sqrt6/6` | 비교 위치. 직접 구현 인용이 아니다.<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3010` `      READ(15,*) A00,B00,C00`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:516` `               GA00DPAvgOAreaIE4 = ALPHAL(IE)*A00*G*DPAvg*Cfac/AreaIE4`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:538` `               MsFacLOnDiag =OnDiag*AreaIE*(1.d0/DT+Tau0Avg_S/2.d0)/DT/12.d0` | 문서에만 | 해석: 원문의 2D consistent·선형 요소·등간격·일정 수심 조건을 보존했다. 주 코드에서 이 조건의 자동 검사를 찾지 못했다. |
| `TIME-GWCE-UNCOND`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:94` `Kinnmark's 1986 monograph [5]_ does offer some analysis that backs up this`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:95` `empirical experience. For the consistent mass-matrix solver employing the`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:96` ``centered :math:`\theta` scheme it can be shown that unconditional stability is``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:97` ``only possible if :math:`\theta \geq 0.5` and,``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:99` `` :math:`\tau_0 \leq \tau` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:101` ``where :math:`\tau` is the linear friction coefficient. In the deep ocean when`` | `\theta\ge0.5,\ \tau_0\le\tau` | 비교 위치. 직접 구현 인용이 아니다.<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3010` `      READ(15,*) A00,B00,C00`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:516` `               GA00DPAvgOAreaIE4 = ALPHAL(IE)*A00*G*DPAvg*Cfac/AreaIE4`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:538` `               MsFacLOnDiag =OnDiag*AreaIE*(1.d0/DT+Tau0Avg_S/2.d0)/DT/12.d0` | 문서에만 | 해석: 원문은 consistent·중앙 시간 가중치·선형 마찰 분석에서 only possible if라고 적는다. 이를 충분조건으로 바꾸지 않았다. 현재 입력은 τ₀≤τ를 필수 검사하지 않는다. |
| `TIME-GWCE-CR-CI`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:109` `It can be shown that this is the case in 1-D when,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:111` ``-  :math:`Cr < 2\sqrt{3}/3`, when :math:`\theta \geq 0.5` (consistent``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:112` `   mass-matrix)` | `Cr<2\sqrt3/3,\ \theta\ge0.5` | 비교 위치. 직접 구현 인용이 아니다.<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3010` `      READ(15,*) A00,B00,C00`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:516` `               GA00DPAvgOAreaIE4 = ALPHAL(IE)*A00*G*DPAvg*Cfac/AreaIE4`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:538` `               MsFacLOnDiag =OnDiag*AreaIE*(1.d0/DT+Tau0Avg_S/2.d0)/DT/12.d0` | 문서에만 | 해석: 원문의 1D consistent 조건에서 τ₀ 선택 제한을 없애는 해석 경계이다. 자동 코드 선택식이 아니다. |
| `TIME-GWCE-CR-CE`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:109` `It can be shown that this is the case in 1-D when,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:113` ``-  :math:`Cr < \sqrt{3}/3`, when :math:`\theta = 0` (consistent mass-matrix)`` | `Cr<\sqrt3/3,\ \theta=0` | 비교 위치. 직접 구현 인용이 아니다.<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3010` `      READ(15,*) A00,B00,C00`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:516` `               GA00DPAvgOAreaIE4 = ALPHAL(IE)*A00*G*DPAvg*Cfac/AreaIE4`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:538` `               MsFacLOnDiag =OnDiag*AreaIE*(1.d0/DT+Tau0Avg_S/2.d0)/DT/12.d0` | 문서에만 | 해석: 원문의 1D consistent·명시 조건을 보존했다. 자동 코드 선택식이 아니다. |
| `TIME-GWCE-CR-LI`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:109` `It can be shown that this is the case in 1-D when,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:114` ``-  :math:`Cr < 2`, when :math:`\theta \geq 0.5` (lumped mass-matrix)`` | `Cr<2,\ \theta\ge0.5` | 비교 위치. 직접 구현 인용이 아니다.<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3010` `      READ(15,*) A00,B00,C00`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:516` `               GA00DPAvgOAreaIE4 = ALPHAL(IE)*A00*G*DPAvg*Cfac/AreaIE4`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:538` `               MsFacLOnDiag =OnDiag*AreaIE*(1.d0/DT+Tau0Avg_S/2.d0)/DT/12.d0` | 문서에만 | 해석: 원문의 1D lumped 조건을 보존했다. 별도의 일반 구현 허용값으로 판정하지 않았다. |
| `TIME-GWCE-CR-LE`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:109` `It can be shown that this is the case in 1-D when,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:115` ``-  :math:`Cr < 1`, when :math:`\theta = 0` (lumped mass-matrix)`` | `Cr<1,\ \theta=0` | 비교 위치. 직접 구현 인용이 아니다.<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3010` `      READ(15,*) A00,B00,C00`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:516` `               GA00DPAvgOAreaIE4 = ALPHAL(IE)*A00*G*DPAvg*Cfac/AreaIE4`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:538` `               MsFacLOnDiag =OnDiag*AreaIE*(1.d0/DT+Tau0Avg_S/2.d0)/DT/12.d0` | 문서에만 | 해석: 원문의 1D lumped·명시 조건을 보존했다. 자동 코드 선택식이 아니다. |
| `TIME-NONCENTER`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:128` `One aspect missing from the theory above are different weighting schemes which`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:129` ``are possibly non-centered, i.e., :math:`\mathrm{A00} \neq \mathrm{C00}`. Pringle``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:130` `et al. [7]_ shows that stability of the GWCE is improved if,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:132` `` :math:`\mathrm{A00} = \mathrm{B00} = 0.5\theta, \quad \mathrm{C00} = 1-\theta` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:134` `which is unconditionally stable under the following conditions for the`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:135` `consistent mass-matrix solver,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:137` `` :math:`2/3 \leq \theta \leq 1, \quad \tau_0\Delta t \leq (16/3)(3\theta/2-1)` `` | `A00=B00=0.5\theta,\ C00=1-\theta;\quad 2/3\le\theta\le1,\ \tau_0\Delta t\le(16/3)(3\theta/2-1)` | 비교 위치. 직접 구현 인용이 아니다.<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3010` `      READ(15,*) A00,B00,C00`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:516` `               GA00DPAvgOAreaIE4 = ALPHAL(IE)*A00*G*DPAvg*Cfac/AreaIE4`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:538` `               MsFacLOnDiag =OnDiag*AreaIE*(1.d0/DT+Tau0Avg_S/2.d0)/DT/12.d0` | 문서에만 | 해석: 비중앙 시간 가중치와 consistent 안정성의 해석식이다. 코드에는 A00≠C00를 읽는 경로가 있다. 이 부등식 검사나 θ 자동 선택은 주 코드에서 찾지 못했다. |
| `TIME-DOC-TAU-TYPO`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:99` `` :math:`\tau_0 \leq \tau` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:123` ``in the GWCE is to choose a sufficiently small :math:`\tau_0 \leq \tau`.``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:139` `thus removing the restrictive condition in the centered-scheme`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:140` ``(:math:`\tau_0 \leq \Delta t`), instead requiring only that :math:`\tau_0` is``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/a00_b00_c00.rst:141` ``small enough for the given time step :math:`\Delta t`.`` | `\tau_0\le\Delta t` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:2500` `      READ(15,*) Tau0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:2521` `      READ(15,*) DTDP` | 불일치 | 해석: 문서의 이 줄은 중앙 방식 제한을 τ₀≤Δt로 적는다. 같은 파일의 앞선 식은 τ₀≤τ이다. 코드의 τ₀와 시간 간격은 별도 입력이다. 해석: 1/시간과 시간의 비교를 적은 문서 내부 표기 오류이다. 실행 안정성을 시험한 판정은 아니다. |

#### τ₀

| 대조 ID·문서 파일:줄·원문 | 식 전사·비교용 대응 | 구현 코드 또는 비교 코드 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `TAU-ZERO-POS`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:28` ``Because the :ref:`TAU0` value specified in the fort.15 file can be either a flag``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:29` ``(indicating how ADCIRC should operate) or the actual :math:`\tau_0` value used``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:30` `in solving the GWCE, it is important to distinguish between the two. All`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:31` ``negative :ref:`TAU0` are flags, all positive :ref:`TAU0` are :math:`\tau_0`.``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:32` ``Furthermore, some values are overridden by the :ref:`primitive weighting in``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:33` ``continuity equation <primitive_weighting_in_continuity_equation>` nodal`` | `\tau_0=TAU0\quad(TAU0\ge0)` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1910` `         IF (Tau0.ge.0) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1912` `              Tau0Var(I)=Tau0` | 일치 | 해석: 절점 속성으로 덮어쓰지 않을 때 비음수 입력을 그대로 사용한다. 코드는 TAU0=0도 같은 분기에 넣는다. 문서의 positive 표현에는 이 경계값 설명이 없다. |
| `TAU-MINMAX-TABLE`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:101` `   * - Minimum`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:103` `     - 0.002`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:104` `     - 0.002`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:109` `   * - Maximum`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:111` `     - 0.005`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:112` `     - 1` | `TAU0=-1,-2:\ \min=0.002;\quad TAU0=-1:\ \max=0.005` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2151` `         IF(Depth.GE.200.) Tau0NodalValue=0.005`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2153` `            Tau0NodalValue=1./Depth`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2158` `         IF(Depth.LE.10.) Tau0NodalValue=0.020d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2159` `         IF(Depth.GT.10.) Tau0NodalValue=0.005d0` | 불일치 | 해석: 값표의 −1·−2 최소 0.002를 코드에서 얻지 못한다. 양수 수심에서 두 분기의 최소값은 0.005이다. −1 최대값은 코드에서 0.020이다. 문서 값표와 문서 본문도 서로 다르다. |
| `TAU-1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:200` ```\| \*\ ``TAU0 = -1``, :math:`\tau_0` is computed as follows:```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:204` ```    -  ``If depth >= 10; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 0.005`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:205` ```    -  ``If depth < 10; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 0.020`` ``` | `D\ge10:\tau_0=0.005;\quad D<10:\tau_0=0.020` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2158` `         IF(Depth.LE.10.) Tau0NodalValue=0.020d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2159` `         IF(Depth.GT.10.) Tau0NodalValue=0.005d0` | 불일치 | 해석: 문서는 D≥10에서 0.005를 요구한다. 코드는 D≤10에서 0.020을 쓴다. D=10에서 계수와 경계 조건이 다르다. |
| `TAU-2`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:210` ```-  ``TAU0 = -2``, :math:`\tau_0` is is computed according to depth as follows:```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:212` ```    -  ``If depth >= 200; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 0.005`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:213` ```    -  ``If 1 < depth < 200; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 1/depth`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:214` ```    -  ``If depth < 1; then``\ :math:`\tau_0 = 1` ``` | `D\ge200:\tau_0=0.005;\ 1<D<200:\tau_0=1/D;\ D<1:\tau_0=1` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2149` `      IF (Scheme.eq.-2.d0) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2151` `         IF(Depth.GE.200.) Tau0NodalValue=0.005`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2152` `         IF((Depth.LT.200.).AND.(Depth.GE.1.)) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2155` `         IF(Depth.LT.1.) Tau0NodalValue=1.0` | 불일치 | 해석: D≥200와 D<1의 값은 대응한다. 문서는 D=1을 어떤 분기에도 넣지 않는다. 코드는 1≤D<200에서 1/D를 쓴다. 경계값 1의 조건 차이를 숨기지 않았다. |
| `TAU-FRACTION`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:35` ```their meaning. Note that for ``TAU0 = -x.1`` (where ``x`` is an integer),```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:36` ```behavior is the same as ``-x``, but the :math:`\tau_0` values are written to the```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:37` ``:ref:`fort.90 file <fort90>`. More on this below in`` | `TAU0=-x.1:\ \text{same as }-x` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1924` `               Tau0Var(I)=Tau0NodalValue(Tau0,DP(I))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1989` `      IF ( Tau0Dig2.eq.-1 ) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2149` `      IF (Scheme.eq.-2.d0) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2158` `         IF(Depth.LE.10.) Tau0NodalValue=0.020d0` | 불일치 | 해석: 출력 여부는 소수 자리로 선택한다. 그러나 수심 계수 함수는 Scheme.eq.-2.d0만 검사한다. −2.1은 −2와 같은 1/D 분기를 타지 않는다. 해석: D=100에서 −2는 0.01을 주고 −2.1은 0.005를 준다. 이 값은 실행 결과가 아니라 원문 분기 대입값이다. |
| `TAU-ATTRIBUTE`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:206` ``   -  This value is ignored if `primitive weighting in continuity``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:207` ``      equation <primitive_weighting_in_continuity_equation>`__ is specified in``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:208` `      the fort.15 file.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:215` ``   -  This value is ignored if `primitive weighting in continuity``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:216` ``      equation <primitive_weighting_in_continuity_equation>`__ is specified in``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:217` `      the fort.15 file.` | `\tau_0=\tau_{0,\text{attribute}}` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1910` `         IF (Tau0.ge.0) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1952` `      IF (LoadTau0.and.Tau0DefVal.lt.0) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1954` `            IF (Tau0Var(I).lt.0) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1955` `               Tau0Var(I)=Tau0NodalValue(Tau0DefVal,DP(I))` | 일치 | 해석: LoadTau0가 켜져 있으면 절점 값을 사용한다. 속성의 음수 기본값은 미지정 절점의 수심 분기를 선택한다. 속성 명칭의 상세 연결은 rst를 근거로 삼았다. |
| `TAU-3`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:224` ```-  ``TAU0 = -3``, :math:`\tau_0` is computed from ``TAU0Base`` (read in from the```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:225` ``   `nodal attribute <primitive_weighting_in_continuity_equation>`__) as follows:``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:227` ```   -  ``If TAU0Base < 0.025; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= TAU0Base`` (constant in```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:228` `      time)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:229` ```    -  ``If TAU0Base >= 0.025; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= TAU0Base+1.5*TK`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:230` ```       where ``TK=speed*Cd/depth`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:113` `     - 0.2` | `b<0.025:\tau_0=b;\ b\ge0.025:\tau_0=\min(b+1.5TK,0.2)` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2201` `            IF(Tau0Base(i).lt.0.025) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2202` `               Tau0Temp(I)=Tau0Base(i) ! not time varying`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2204` `               Tau0Temp(i)=Tau0Base(i)+1.5*TK(i) ! time varying`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2205` `               IF (Tau0Temp(i).gt.0.2) Tau0Temp(i)=0.2 ! ceiling` | 일치 | 해석: 본문의 Base 분기와 표의 0.2 상한을 함께 적용하면 계수식이 같다. TK의 현재 텐서 해석은 별도 행에 적었다. |
| `TAU-3-6-MISSING`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:220` `\| In the unlikely event that one of the below options not paired with its`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:221` `  corresponding nodal attribute (the code will exit if this is done for some`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:222` ```  ``TAU0``), then default values are set using the ``TAU0 = -1`` logic.``` | `TAU0=-3.x,-6.x\ \land\neg LoadTau0` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1863` `      IF ( ((Tau0Dig1.eq.-3).or.(Tau0Dig1.eq.-6))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1864` `     &          .and.(.not.LoadTau0) ) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1868` `         CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE,` | 일치 | 해석: 문서는 some TAU0에서 속성이 없으면 종료한다고 적는다. 코드의 구체적 종료 조건은 정수 자리 −3 또는 −6이다. 이 조건을 모든 음수 TAU0의 종료로 바꾸지 않았다. |
| `TAU-5`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:232` ```-  ``TAU0 = -5``, :math:`\tau_0` is computed similar to ``TAU0 = -3`` as```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:235` ```    -  :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= Tau0Min+1.5*TK`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:236` `   -  It is limited by`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:237` ````      ```Tau0FullDomainMin`` <Tau0FullDomainMin>`__\ ``<=``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``<=``\ ```Tau0FullDomainMax`` <Tau0FullDomainMax>`__,````<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:238` ```      which are specified on the line following ``TAU0`` in the fort.15 file```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:239` ```      (only when ``TAU0 = -5``)```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:240` ``   -  If the `min and max primitive weighting in continuity``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:241` ``      equation <min_and_max_primitive_weighting_in_continuity_equation>`__ nodal``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:242` `      attribute is used, then the nodal minimum and maximum values replace the`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:243` `      full-domain values in the above calculation.` | `\tau_0=\min(\max(\tau_{0,\min}+1.5TK,\tau_{0,\min}),\tau_{0,\max})` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:2505` `      IF ( (Tau0.le.-5.d0).and.(Tau0.gt.-6.d0) ) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:2506` `        READ(15,*) Tau0FullDomainMin, Tau0FullDomainMax`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1981` `               Tau0MinMax(I,1) = Tau0FullDomainMin`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1982` `               Tau0MinMax(I,2) = Tau0FullDomainMax`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2190` `            Tau0Temp(i)=Tau0MinMax(i,1)+1.5*TK(i)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2195` `               Tau0Temp(i)=Tau0MinMax(i,2)` | 일치 | 해석: 코드는 −6<TAU0≤−5에서 바로 다음 줄의 전역 최소·최대값을 읽는다. 절점 최소·최대 속성을 읽었으면 그 값을 사용한다. 시간 갱신 뒤 공간 평균을 따로 적용한다. |
| `TAU-6`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:245` ```-  ``TAU0 = -6``, :math:`\tau_0` is defined using the rules for ``TAU0 = -3``,```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:246` `   and then is set equal to the time-average of the current and previous`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:247` `   (time-averaged) values.` | `\tau_0^{new}=0.5\bar\tau_0^{current}+0.5\tau_0^{last}` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2221` `            TAU0VAR(I) = 0.5d0*TAU0VAR(I) + 0.5d0*LastTau0(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2222` `            LastTau0(I) = TAU0VAR(I)` | 일치 | 해석: 공간 평균 뒤 현재 값과 이전의 이미 시간 평균한 값을 각각 1/2로 결합한다. LastTau0를 새 결과로 갱신한다. |
| `TAU-7-WEIGHT`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:248` ```-  ``TAU0 = -7``, :math:`\tau_0` is defined using the rules for ``TAU0 = -3``,```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:249` `   and then is set equal to the weighted time-average of the current and`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:250` `   previous (time-averaged) values as follows:`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:252` ```   -  :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= AlphaTau0*TAU0VAR + (1.0-AlphaTau0)*LastTau0``, where```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:254` ```      ``AlphaTau0 = 0.25`` is hard-coded into the model. This means that```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:255` ``      :math:`\tau_0` is weighted 75% toward older values.`` | `\tau_0^{new}=\alpha\tau_0^{current}+(1-\alpha)\tau_0^{last},\quad\alpha=0.25` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:280` `      REAL(8), PARAMETER :: AlphaTau0 = 0.25d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2229` `            TAU0VAR(I) = AlphaTau0*TAU0VAR(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2230` `     &                 + (1.d0-AlphaTau0)*LastTau0(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2231` `            LastTau0(I) = TAU0VAR(I)` | 일치 | 해석: AlphaTau0 기본값 0.25와 과거 값 0.75의 가중이 같다. LastTau0는 시간 평균한 결과를 저장한다. |
| `TAU-7-VAR-DESC`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:252` ```   -  :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= AlphaTau0*TAU0VAR + (1.0-AlphaTau0)*LastTau0``, where```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:253` ```      ``TAU0VAR`` is ``Tau0Base`` after spatial averaging, and``` | `TAU0VAR=\text{Tau0Base after spatial averaging}` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2204` `               Tau0Temp(i)=Tau0Base(i)+1.5*TK(i) ! time varying`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2213` `            CaseySum = CaseySum + Tau0Temp(NeiTab(I,J))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2215` `         TAU0VAR(I) = CaseySum / NNeigh(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2229` `            TAU0VAR(I) = AlphaTau0*TAU0VAR(I)` | 불일치 | 해석: 현재 공간 평균 대상은 Tau0Base 자체가 아니라 Tau0Temp이다. Base≥0.025이면 Tau0Temp에는 1.5TK와 0.2 상한이 들어간다. 문서 변수 설명이 이 계수 계산을 생략한다. |
| `TAU-TK-TENSOR`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:229` ```    -  ``If TAU0Base >= 0.025; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= TAU0Base+1.5*TK`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:230` ```       where ``TK=speed*Cd/depth`` ``` | `TK=\text{speed}\,C_d/\text{depth}` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:442` `               TK(I) = max(abs(TKM(1,I))+abs(TKM(3,I)),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:443` `     &                     abs(TKM(2,I))+abs(TKM(3,I)))` | 불일치 | 해석: 현재 2DDI 시간 갱신의 TK는 TKM의 최대 절대 행합이다. 비등방 내부파 항력과 추가 마찰이 없으면 문서의 등방식과 대응한다. 일반적인 텐서 조건에서는 문서 식과 다르다. |
| `TAU-UPDATE`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:262` ```For ``TAU0 = -3, -5, -6, or -7``, :math:`\tau_0` is updated (via the```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:263` ``` ``CalculateTimeVaryingTau0`` subroutine) in space and in time. An initial ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:264` `"update" is done when the model starts. After this, updates are done only after`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:265` `a time step in which there is a change in wet/dry state somewhere in the model`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:270` ``applied during the update. Each node's :math:`\tau_0` is then spatially averaged``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:271` ```with all immediate neighbors. Time-averaging (for ``TAU0 = -6 or -7``) is```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:272` `applied last.` | `NCChange>0\quad\text{or}\quad CGWCE_HDP` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:427` `      NCIFBLCK: IF(NCChange.GT.0 .or. CGWCE_HDP ) THEN !if any subdomain grid has changed`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:445` `            CALL CalculateTimeVaryingTau0(TK, NNeigh, NeiTab, NP)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2212` `         DO J=1,NNeigh(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2215` `         TAU0VAR(I) = CaseySum / NNeigh(I)` | 불일치 | 해석: 문서는 초기화 뒤 only after wet/dry change라고 적는다. 코드의 갱신 진입 조건에는 CGWCE_HDP도 있다. CGWCE_HDP가 참이면 wet/dry 변화가 없어도 진입한다. 공간 평균과 마지막 시간 평균의 순서는 대응한다. |

#### 바닥 마찰

| 대조 ID·문서 파일:줄·원문 | 식 전사·비교용 대응 | 구현 코드 또는 비교 코드 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `FRICTION-0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst:31` ``     - uses a spatially constant linear bottom friction, :math:`\tau_b` of dimensions [1/T]`` | `TK=\tau` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2025` `         IFLINBF=1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2468` `            TK(I) = MIN(FRIC(I)*`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2470` `     &                     (UV1/H1) * (IFNLBF +                               ! nonlinear`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2471` `     &                     IFHYBF*(1+(HBREAK/H1)**FTHETA)**(FGAMMA/FTHETA)) ), ! hybrid `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2472` `     &                  2.0/DTDP)` | 일치 | 해석: NOLIBF=0이 IFLINBF 분기를 선택한다. 계수와 지수는 대응한다. 2/DTDP 상한이 작동하지 않는 조건에서 비교했다. |
| `FRICTION-1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst:35` ``      - can specify a spatially constant or spatially varying dimensionless coefficient, :math:`C_f`; in which :math:`\tau_b = C_f\|U\|/H` `` | `TK=C_f\|U\|/H` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2029` `         IFNLBF=1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2468` `            TK(I) = MIN(FRIC(I)*`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2470` `     &                     (UV1/H1) * (IFNLBF +                               ! nonlinear`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2471` `     &                     IFHYBF*(1+(HBREAK/H1)**FTHETA)**(FGAMMA/FTHETA)) ), ! hybrid `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2472` `     &                  2.0/DTDP)` | 일치 | 해석: NOLIBF=1이 IFNLBF 분기를 선택한다. 계수와 지수는 대응한다. 2/DTDP 상한이 작동하지 않는 조건에서 비교했다. |
| `FRICTION-2`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst:39` `     - In deep water, the friction coefficient is constant and a quadratic bottom friction law results. In shallow water the friction coefficient increases as the depth decreases (e.g. as in a Manning-type friction law). The friction coefficient is determined as: FFACTOR =FFACTORMIN*[1+(HBREAK/H)**FTHETA]**(FGAMMA/FTHETA)` | `TK=\frac{C_{f,\min}\|U\|}{H}[1+(HBREAK/H)^{FTHETA}]^{FGAMMA/FTHETA}` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2036` `         IFHYBF=1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2468` `            TK(I) = MIN(FRIC(I)*`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2470` `     &                     (UV1/H1) * (IFNLBF +                               ! nonlinear`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2471` `     &                     IFHYBF*(1+(HBREAK/H1)**FTHETA)**(FGAMMA/FTHETA)) ), ! hybrid `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2472` `     &                  2.0/DTDP)` | 일치 | 해석: NOLIBF=2이 IFHYBF 분기를 선택한다. 계수와 지수는 대응한다. 2/DTDP 상한이 작동하지 않는 조건에서 비교했다. |
| `FRICTION-ALLOWED`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst:47` ``In the :ref:`NWP <nwp>` section, if the user selects``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst:48` `quadratic_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor, mannings_n_at_sea_floor, or`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst:49` `chezy_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor, then NOLIBF must be 1 (nonlinear`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst:50` `friction formulation) since all those formulations are nonlinear. If the NOLIBF`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst:51` `were anything other than 1, it is an error that will cause ADCIRC to stop.` | 독립 식 없음. 원문 조건·설정 설명을 대조했다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1850` `      IF ((LoadChezy.or.LoadManningsN.or.LoadQuadraticFric).and.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1851` `     &     NoLiBF.ne.1.AND.NOLIBF.NE.3) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:3168` `      IF(NOLIBF.EQ.3) READ(15,*) CF,HBREAK,FTHETA` | 불일치 | 해석: 문서는 세 절점 마찰 속성에 NOLIBF must be 1이며 otherwise stop이라고 적는다. 현재 코드의 오류 조건은 NOLIBF가 1도 3도 아닐 때이다. NOLIBF=3을 허용한다. |
| `MANNING-ALLOWED`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/physics_parameters/mannings_n.rst:22` `If the user elects to use this nodal attribute, which ADCIRC reads in as the`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/physics_parameters/mannings_n.rst:23` ``` ``ManningsN`` variable, :ref:`NOLIBF` must be set to 1 or the run will terminate. ``` | 독립 식 없음. 원문 조건·설정 설명을 대조했다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1850` `      IF ((LoadChezy.or.LoadManningsN.or.LoadQuadraticFric).and.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:1851` `     &     NoLiBF.ne.1.AND.NOLIBF.NE.3) THEN` | 불일치 | 해석: 현행 Manning 설명도 NOLIBF must be 1 or terminate라고 적는다. 코드의 검사에서는 3도 허용한다. 코드 주석과 종료 메시지도 아직 1만 적는다. |
| `MANNING-FORMULA`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/physics_parameters/mannings_n.rst:29` `.. math:: C_d(t)=\frac{gn^2}{\sqrt[3]{h+\eta(t)}}`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/physics_parameters/mannings_n.rst:40` `The addition of the water surface elevation is conditional upon the setting of`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/physics_parameters/mannings_n.rst:41` ```:ref:`NOLIFA <NOLIFA>`: *η* is treated as zero if ``NOLIFA = 0`` in the```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/physics_parameters/mannings_n.rst:42` ``:ref:`fort.15 file <fort15>`. Finally, the value of :ref:`CF <CF>` in the``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/physics_parameters/mannings_n.rst:43` ``:ref:`fort.15 file <fort15>` is used to set a lower limit on the resulting``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/physics_parameters/mannings_n.rst:44` ``equivalent quadratic friction coefficient, under the assumption the *C*\ :sub:`d` calculated from``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/physics_parameters/mannings_n.rst:45` `this formula tends to become too small in deep water.` | `C_d(t)=gn^2/[h+\eta(t)]^{1/3}` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2424` `                  FRIC(I)=g*(WPFac*ManningsN(I))**2.d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2425` `     &                    /( ( HT )**(1.d0/3.d0) ) ! sb46.28sb02`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2427` `                  IF(FRIC(I).LT.BFCdLLimit) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2428` `                     FRIC(I) = BFCdLLimit`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2466` `               H1  = DP(I) + IFNLFA*ETA2(I)` | 일치 | 해석: NOLIFA가 비영이고 WPFac=1인 일반 요소에서 Manning 식이 대응한다. BFCdLLimit 하한이 작동하지 않는 양수 n 조건에서 비교했다. NOLIFA=0이면 코드가 수위를 제외한다. 젖은 둘레 및 장벽 월류 보정은 별도 처리이다. |

#### 유한 진폭·젖음/마름

| 대조 ID·문서 파일:줄·원문 | 식 전사·비교용 대응 | 구현 코드 또는 비교 코드 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `FINITE-AMP-0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolifa.rst:31` `   * - 0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolifa.rst:32` `     - No finite amplitude terms and no wetting-drying`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolifa.rst:33` ``     - The depth is linearized by using the bathymetric depth, rather than the total depth, in all terms except the transient term in the continuity equation. Wetting and drying of elements is disabled. Initial water depths are assumed equal to the bathymetric water depth specified in the `fort.14 file <fort.14_file>`__.`` | `H=h,\quad IFNLFA=0` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:286` `      IF(NOLIFA.EQ.0) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:287` `         IFNLFA=0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2466` `               H1  = DP(I) + IFNLFA*ETA2(I)` | 일치 | 해석: NOLIFA=0에서 IFNLFA=0으로 총 수심의 수위 기여를 없앤다. 초기 수심과 초기화 지시는 기본 cold start 조건에서 비교했다. |
| `FINITE-AMP-1-2`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolifa.rst:35` `   * - 1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolifa.rst:36` `     - Finite amplitude terms without wetting-drying`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolifa.rst:37` ``     - Finite amplitude terms are included in the model run and wetting and drying of elements is disabled. Initial water depths are assumed equal to the bathymetric water depth specified in the :ref:`fort.14 file <fort14>`.``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolifa.rst:39` `   * - 2`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolifa.rst:40` `     - Finite amplitude terms with wetting-drying`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolifa.rst:41` ``     - Finite amplitude terms are included in the model run and wetting and drying of elements is enabled. Initial water depths are assumed equal to the bathymetric water depth specified in the :ref:`fort.14 file <fort14>`.``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolifa.rst:48` `When the finite amplitude terms are turned on, the time derivative portion of`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolifa.rst:49` `the advective terms should also be turned on for proper mass conservation and`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolifa.rst:50` ``consistency (i.e., when NOLIFA > 0, then `NOLICA <NOLICA>`__ = 1).`` | `H=h+\zeta,\quad IFNLFA=1` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:288` `      ELSE`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:289` `         IFNLFA=1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:241` `               IF(HTOT.LE.H0) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:274` `                     NNODECODE(I) = 0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wetdry.F:440` `                     IF(VEL.GT.VELMIN) THEN` | 일치 | 해석: NOLIFA=1,2에서 유한 진폭을 사용한다. 젖음/마름 처리는 활성 조건에서 수심과 유속으로 판정한다. 원문 should NOLICA=1을 일반 강제 종료 규칙으로 바꾸지 않았다. |

#### IM

| 대조 ID·문서 파일:줄·원문 | 식 전사·비교용 대응 | 구현 코드 또는 비교 코드 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `IM-MASS`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst:101` ```      - Consistent (implicit for linear part of gravity wave term), barotropic ``ILump=0`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst:108` ```      - Lumped (explicit), barotropic ``CGWCE_Lump=.TRUE.``, ``ILump=1`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst:115` ```      - Consistent (implicit for full gravity wave term), barotropic ``CGWCE_HDP=.TRUE.``, ``IFNL_HDP=1``, ``ILump=0`` ``` | `ILump=0;\ ILump=1;\ CGWCE_HDP,\ IFNL_HDP=1` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:1350` `            ILump=0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:1353` `            ILump=1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:1359` `            CGWCE_HDP = .TRUE.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:1360` `            IFNL_HDP = 1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:2003` `         CALL JCG(NP,MNP,MNEI,NEITAB,COEF,GWCE_LV,ETAS,` | 일치 | 해석: consistent·lumped 선택은 풀이 경로에 대응한다. HDP 선택은 선형 수심 h 대신 총 수심 H를 새 수위 강성항에 사용한다. |
| `IM-LATERAL`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst:96` ```      - 2DDI, Kolar-Gray flux-based ``CGWCE_LS_KGQ=.TRUE.`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst:103` ```      - 2DDI, 2-part flux-based ``CGWCE_LS_2PartQ=.TRUE.`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst:110` ```      - 2DDI, 2-part velocity-based ``CGWCE_LS_2PartV=.TRUE.`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst:112` ```      - Integration by parts, velocity-based symmetrical ``CME_LS_IBPSV=.TRUE.`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst:117` ```      - 2DDI, 2-part flux-based symmetrical ``CGWCE_LS_2PartSQ=.TRUE.`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst:119` ```      - Integration by parts, flux-based symmetrical ``CME_LS_IBPSQ=.TRUE.`` ``` | `KGQ,\ 2PartQ,\ 2PartV,\ IBPSV,\ IBPSQ` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:1252` `            CGWCE_LS_2PartQ  = .TRUE.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:1256` `            CGWCE_LS_2PartV  = .TRUE.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/momentum.F:513` `         IF (CME_LS_IBPSQ) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/momentum.F:558` `         IF (CME_LS_IBPSV) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1253` `         IF(CGWCE_LS_KGQ) THEN` | 일치 | 해석: 플래그 선택은 실제 KG·응력 분기에 대응한다. 이 행은 선택 의미를 판정한다. 대칭 응력의 계수 차이는 PDF 식 (2.5)·(2.6)에 적었다. |
| `IM-AREA`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst:100` ```      - Corrected ``CME_AreaInt_Corr=.TRUE.`` ```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst:107` ```      - Original ``CME_AreaInt_Orig=.TRUE.`` ``` | 독립 식 없음. 원문 조건·설정 설명을 대조했다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/read_input.F:1343` `         ELSEIF(IMDig5.EQ.2) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/momentum.F:720` `            IF (CME_AreaInt_Orig) THEN       !Original (incorrect) area integration`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/momentum.F:721` `               MOM_LV_X(I)=MOM_LV_X(I)/MJU(I)` | 일치 | 해석: CME_AreaInt_Corr와 CME_AreaInt_Orig 선택이 코드에 남아 있다. 원래 면적 적분이 제거되었다는 2004년 부록 문장과 구분했다. |

#### 수평 점성

| 대조 ID·문서 파일:줄·원문 | 식 전사·비교용 대응 | 구현 코드 또는 비교 코드 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `KG-VISC-GRAD`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/physics_parameters/horizontal_eddy_viscosity.rst:89` `It is also set for any regular IM value for 2D ADCIRC. Mathematically, the`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/physics_parameters/horizontal_eddy_viscosity.rst:90` `Kolar-Gray formulation of the lateral stress does not take into account a`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/physics_parameters/horizontal_eddy_viscosity.rst:91` `derivative of the eddy viscosity terms, meaning it is technically inconsistent`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/physics_parameters/horizontal_eddy_viscosity.rst:92` `with a Smagorinsky turbulence closure model. However, it is not clear whether` | `\nabla E_h\cdot\nabla Q` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1254` `            EVMXGrad=(EVMN1*FDX1+EVMN2*FDX2+EVMN3*FDX3)/AreaIE2`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1255` `            EVMYGrad=(EVMN1*FDY1+EVMN2*FDY2+EVMN3*FDY3)/AreaIE2`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1257` `            MX=(EVMXGrad*(QX1N1*FDX1+QX1N2*FDX2+QX1N3*FDX3)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/gwce.F:1258` `     &         +EVMYGrad*(QY1N1*FDX1+QY1N2*FDX2+QY1N3*FDX3)` | 불일치 | 해석: 현행 문서는 KG가 점성계수의 미분을 고려하지 않는다고 적는다. 현재 KG 분기는 EVMXGrad·EVMYGrad와 유량 기울기의 곱을 계산한다. 이 직접 차이를 Smagorinsky 전체 안정성 판정으로 확대하지 않았다. |

#### 바람 응력

| 대조 ID·문서 파일:줄·원문 | 식 전사·비교용 대응 | 구현 코드 또는 비교 코드 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `WIND-GARRATT`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst:155` `This is the default wind drag formulation in ADCIRC. From Garratt (1977) [4]_,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst:158` `.. math:: c_d=0.001 \left ( 0.75+0.067 \left \Vert \mathbf{w} \right \\| \right )`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst:160` `By default, ADCIRC puts an upper bound on the drag coefficient of`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst:161` ```:math:`c_d\le0.0035`. This upper bound ``WindDragLimit`` can be changed via the```<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst:162` `` `metControl <metControl>`__ fort.15 namelist. `` | `c_d=\min\{0.001(0.75+0.067\|w\|),0.0035\}` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:122` `      REAL(8) :: WindDragLimit = 0.0035d0   ! maximum wind drag value`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:442` `              f_computeWindDrag => CappedGarrattWindDrag`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:520` `           Drag = 0.001d0*(0.75d0+0.067d0*WindSpeed)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:537` `           WindDrag = MIN(GarrattWindDrag(WindSpeed,NodeNumber),WindDragLimit)` | 일치 | 해석: 기본 함수 포인터는 CappedGarrattWindDrag이다. Garratt의 절편·기울기·기본 상한이 대응한다. WindDragLimit를 바꾸면 상한은 입력값이다. |
| `WIND-Z0-MARINE`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst:65` `.. math:: z_{0m}=\frac{0.018}{g} c_d \left \Vert \mathbf{w} \right \\|`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst:67` ``for Charnock parameter :math:`0.018`, drag coefficient :math:`c_d` and`` | `z_{0m}=(0.018/g)c_d\|w\|` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2950` `        z0m=(0.018d0/G)*WindDragCo*WindMag**2.d0` | 불일치 | 해석: 문서 식은 풍속의 1승이다. 코드는 WindMag**2.d0를 사용한다. 0.018/g와 Cd는 대응한다. 지수 차이를 원문대로 남겼다. |
| `WIND-REDUCTION`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst:55` `.. math:: f=\left ( \frac{z_{0l}}{z_{0m}}\right ) ^{0.0706} \left ( \frac{\ln \frac{10}{z_{0l}}}{\ln \frac{10}{z_{0m}}} \right )`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/wind_stress.rst:60` `.. math:: \mathbf{w}'=f\mathbf{w}=f [u,v]` | `f=(z_{0l}/z_{0m})^{0.0706}\ln(10/z_{0l})/\ln(10/z_{0m}),\quad w'=fw` | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2971` `        if(z0l.gt.z0m) then`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2972` `           fr=min(1.0D0,(z0l/z0m)**0.0706d0 * log(10.d0/z0l) / log(10.d0/z0m))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2978` `        WindX = fr*WindX`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/nodalattr.F:2979` `        WindY = fr*WindY` | 일치 | 해석: z0l>z0m이고 계산한 f≤1인 조건에서 지수와 로그비가 같다. 그 밖에는 코드가 f≤1로 제한한다. 이 제한은 식 바깥의 추가 처리이다. |

#### 조석 포텐셜

| 대조 ID·문서 파일:줄·원문 | 식 전사·비교용 대응 | 구현 코드 또는 비교 코드 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `TIDE-NTIP-0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/ntip.rst:28` `   * - 0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/ntip.rst:29` `     - No Astronomical Forcing`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/ntip.rst:31` ``     - :ref:`NTIF <ntif_parameter>` = 0`` | 독립 식 없음. 원문 조건·설정 설명을 대조했다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/timestep.F:1521` `         IF(CTIP) THEN` | 일치 | 해석: NTIP=0은 전통적 포텐셜 합을 활성화하지 않는다. NTIF=0은 문서의 동반 입력 조건이다. |
| `TIDE-NTIP-1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/ntip.rst:32` `   * - 1`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/ntip.rst:33` `     - Astronomical Tidal Potential`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/ntip.rst:34` `     - Reconstructs the tidal elevation using the analytical formulation for the equilibrium tidal potential [1]_`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/ntip.rst:35` ``     - :ref:`NTIF <ntif_parameter>` > 0``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/ntip.rst:49` `   Eq. (27), page 17 in Luettich, R.A., Westerink, J.J., 1992. ADCIRC: an`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/ntip.rst:50` `   advanced three-dimensional circulation model for shelves coasts and`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/ntip.rst:51` `   estuaries, report 1: theory and methodology of ADCIRC-2DDI and ADCIRC-3DL,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/ntip.rst:52` `   Dredging Research Program. Vicksburg, MS.` | 독립 식 없음. 원문 조건·설정 설명을 대조했다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/adcirc.F:303` `            L_N(0,I) = 1.5d0*COS(SFEA(I))*COS(SFEA(I)) - 1d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/timestep.F:1540` `                  TPMUL=RampTip*ETRF(J)*TPK(J)*FFT(J)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/timestep.F:1558` `                        TIP2(I) = TIP2(I) + TPMUL * L_N(NA,I) * COS(ARGTP)` | 불일치 | 해석: NTIP=1의 합성 경로는 존재한다. 그러나 현행 문서가 연결한 1992년 식 (27)의 j=0 위도 함수는 코드와 다르다. 코드의 보정된 위도 함수를 함께 적어야 한다. |
| `TIDE-NTIP-2`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/ntip.rst:36` `   * - 2`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/ntip.rst:37` `     - Astronomical Tidal Potential plus Self-attraction and Loading (SAL) Tide [2]_`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/ntip.rst:38` ``     - Reconstructs the tidal elevation by summing the contribution from the analytical formulation for the equilibrium tidal potential [1]_ with the contribution from the prescribed SAL constituent values found in the `fort.24 file <fort.24_file>`__.``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/ntip.rst:39` ``      - :ref:`NTIF <ntif_parameter>` > 0, :ref:`fort.24 file <fort24>` `` | 독립 식 없음. 원문 조건·설정 설명을 대조했다. | `models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/timestep.F:1558` `                        TIP2(I) = TIP2(I) + TPMUL * L_N(NA,I) * COS(ARGTP)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/timestep.F:1559` `     &                            + SALTMUL * SALTAMP(J,I) * COS(ARGSALT)` | 일치 | 해석: 전통적 포텐셜 합에 입력 SAL 진폭·위상의 합을 더한다. 자기 인력·하중의 천문 모델 내부를 재현한 계산으로 판정하지 않았다. |

#### 3D 설정·난류

| 대조 ID·문서 파일:줄·원문 | 식 전사·비교용 대응 | 구현 코드 또는 비교 코드 원문 | 판정 | 이유 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
